TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup over sequential index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup over sequential searching with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Result Types\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n    num_workers: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(args: tuple) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, start_idx = args\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n\n\ndef _merge_vocabularies(vocab_list: list[set]) -> set:\n    \"\"\"Merge multiple vocabulary sets.\"\"\"\n    result = set()\n    for vocab in vocab_list:\n        result.update(vocab)\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_doc_data(data_list: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge multiple document data dictionaries.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for data in data_list:\n        result.update(data)\n    return result\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: Optional[int] = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Split documents into chunks for parallel processing\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel: tokenize and compute TF\n    3. Merge results and compute global document frequencies\n    4. Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    5. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (sequential - requires merged data)\n    6. Compute document vector norms (sequential - fast operation)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Adjust chunk size based on total documents to ensure good load balancing\n    # We want at least 2x num_workers chunks for good distribution\n    min_chunks = num_workers * 2\n    adjusted_chunk_size = max(1, len(documents) // min_chunks)\n    if chunk_size > adjusted_chunk_size and len(documents) > min_chunks:\n        chunk_size = adjusted_chunk_size\n\n    # Step 1: Split documents into chunks\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunk = documents[i : i + chunk_size]\n        chunks.append((chunk, i))\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel\n    # Use chunksize=1 to ensure all workers are utilized\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all workers\n    all_doc_term_freqs = []\n    all_doc_terms = []\n    all_vocabularies = []\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in results:\n        all_doc_term_freqs.append(doc_term_freqs)\n        all_doc_terms.append(doc_terms)\n        all_vocabularies.append(vocabulary)\n\n    # Merge all data\n    doc_term_freqs = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_term_freqs)\n    doc_terms = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_terms)\n    index.vocabulary = _merge_vocabularies(all_vocabularies)\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies\n    # This is fast enough to do sequentially\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores\n    # IDF(t) = log(N / DF(t)) + 1 (smoothed)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n        num_workers=num_workers,\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args: tuple) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    # Using a manual sort with heapq.nlargest equivalent\n    scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    top_results = scores[:top_k]\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    #    documents: list[Document], num_workers: Optional[int] = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Split documents into chunks for parallel processing\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel: tokenize and compute TF\n    3. Merge results and compute global document frequencies\n    4. Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    5. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (sequential - requires merged data)\n    6. Compute document vector norms (sequential - fast operation)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Adjust chunk size based on total documents to ensure good load balancing\n    # We want at least 2x num_workers chunks for good distribution\n    min_chunks = num_workers * 2\n    adjusted_chunk_size = max(1, len(documents) // min_chunks)\n    if chunk_size > adjusted_chunk_size and len(documents) > min_chunks:\n        chunk_size = adjusted_chunk_size\n\n    # Step 1: Split documents into chunks\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunk = documents[i : i + chunk_size]\n        chunks.append((chunk, i))\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel\n    # Use chunksize=1 to ensure all workers are utilized\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all workers\n    all_doc_term_freqs = []\n    all_doc_terms = []\n    all_vocabularies = []\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in results:\n        all_doc_term_freqs.append(doc_term_freqs)\n        all_doc_terms.append(doc_terms)\n        all_vocabularies.append(vocabulary)\n\n    # Merge all data\n    doc_term_freqs = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_term_freqs)\n    doc_terms = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_terms)\n    index.vocabulary = _merge_vocabularies(all_vocabularies)\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies\n    # This is fast enough to do sequentially\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores\n    # IDF(t) = log(N / DF(t)) + 1 (smoothed)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n        num_workers=num_workers,\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args: tuple) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to search a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    # Using a manual sort with heapq.nlargest equivalent\n    scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    top_results = scores[:top_k]\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # For small batches, parallelization overhead is too high\n    # Use sequential processing if we have fewer queries than workers\n    if len(queries) < num_workers * 2:\n        # Fall back to sequential processing\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n        results = [\n            _search_single_query((query, index, top_k, doc_titles))\n            for query in queries\n        ]\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return (results, elapsed)\n\n    # Prepare document titles\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Prepare arguments for each query\n    args_list = [(query, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n\n    # Process queries in parallel\n    # Use chunksize to reduce overhead for large batches\n    chunksize = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 4))\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query, args_list, chunksize=chunksize)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n    from sequential import (\n        batch_search_sequential,\n        build_tfidf_index_sequential,\n        search_sequential,\n    )\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--query\",\n        type=str,\n        default=\"machine learning algorithm\",\n        help=\"Search query\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--benchmark\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Run benchmark comparison\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup over sequential index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup over sequential searching with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import partial\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Result Types\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n    num_workers: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(args: tuple) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, start_idx = args\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n\n\ndef _merge_vocabularies(vocab_list: list[set]) -> set:\n    \"\"\"Merge multiple vocabulary sets.\"\"\"\n    result = set()\n    for vocab in vocab_list:\n        result.update(vocab)\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_doc_data(data_list: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge multiple document data dictionaries.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for data in data_list:\n        result.update(data)\n    return result\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: Optional[int] = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Split documents into chunks for parallel processing\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel: tokenize and compute TF\n    3. Merge results and compute global document frequencies\n    4. Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    5. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (sequential - requires merged data)\n    6. Compute document vector norms (sequential - fast operation)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Adjust chunk size based on total documents to ensure good load balancing\n    # We want at least 2x num_workers chunks for good distribution\n    min_chunks = num_workers * 2\n    adjusted_chunk_size = max(1, len(documents) // min_chunks)\n    if chunk_size > adjusted_chunk_size and len(documents) > min_chunks:\n        chunk_size = adjusted_chunk_size\n\n    # Step 1: Split documents into chunks\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunk = documents[i : i + chunk_size]\n        chunks.append((chunk, i))\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel\n    # Use chunksize=1 to ensure all workers are utilized\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all workers\n    all_doc_term_freqs = []\n    all_doc_terms = []\n    all_vocabularies = []\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in results:\n        all_doc_term_freqs.append(doc_term_freqs)\n        all_doc_terms.append(doc_terms)\n        all_vocabularies.append(vocabulary)\n\n    # Merge all data\n    doc_term_freqs = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_term_freqs)\n    doc_terms = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_terms)\n    index.vocabulary = _merge_vocabularies(all_vocabularies)\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies\n    # This is fast enough to do sequentially\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores\n    # IDF(t) = log(N / DF(t)) + 1 (smoothed)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n        num_workers=num_workers,\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_simple(\n    query: str, idf: dict, inverted_index: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, top_k: int, doc_titles: dict\n) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Simplified worker function to search a single query with minimal serialization overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n        idf: IDF scores dictionary\n        inverted_index: Inverted index\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        top_k: Number of top results\n        doc_titles: Document titles\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    top_results = scores[:top_k]\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # For small batches, parallelization overhead is too high\n    # Use sequential processing if we have fewer queries than workers\n    if len(queries) < num_workers * 2:\n        # Fall back to sequential processing\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n        results = [\n            _search_single_query((query, index, top_k, doc_titles))\n            for query in queries\n        ]\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return (results, elapsed)\n\n    # Prepare document titles\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Prepare arguments for each query\n    args_list = [(query, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n\n    # Process queries in parallel\n    # Use chunksize to reduce overhead for large batches\n    chunksize = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 4))\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_search_single_query, args_list, chunksize=chunksize)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n    from sequential import (\n        batch_search_sequential,\n        build_tfidf_index_sequential,\n        search_sequential,\n    )\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--query\",\n        type=str,\n        default=\"machine learning algorithm\",\n        help=\"Search query\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--benchmark\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Run benchmark comparison\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup over sequential index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup over sequential searching with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import partial\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Result Types\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n    num_workers: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(args: tuple) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, start_idx = args\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n\n\ndef _merge_vocabularies(vocab_list: list[set]) -> set:\n    \"\"\"Merge multiple vocabulary sets.\"\"\"\n    result = set()\n    for vocab in vocab_list:\n        result.update(vocab)\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_doc_data(data_list: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge multiple document data dictionaries.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for data in data_list:\n        result.update(data)\n    return result\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: Optional[int] = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Split documents into chunks for parallel processing\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel: tokenize and compute TF\n    3. Merge results and compute global document frequencies\n    4. Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    5. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (sequential - requires merged data)\n    6. Compute document vector norms (sequential - fast operation)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Adjust chunk size based on total documents to ensure good load balancing\n    # We want at least 2x num_workers chunks for good distribution\n    min_chunks = num_workers * 2\n    adjusted_chunk_size = max(1, len(documents) // min_chunks)\n    if chunk_size > adjusted_chunk_size and len(documents) > min_chunks:\n        chunk_size = adjusted_chunk_size\n\n    # Step 1: Split documents into chunks\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunk = documents[i : i + chunk_size]\n        chunks.append((chunk, i))\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel\n    # Use chunksize=1 to ensure all workers are utilized\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all workers\n    all_doc_term_freqs = []\n    all_doc_terms = []\n    all_vocabularies = []\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in results:\n        all_doc_term_freqs.append(doc_term_freqs)\n        all_doc_terms.append(doc_terms)\n        all_vocabularies.append(vocabulary)\n\n    # Merge all data\n    doc_term_freqs = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_term_freqs)\n    doc_terms = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_terms)\n    index.vocabulary = _merge_vocabularies(all_vocabularies)\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies\n    # This is fast enough to do sequentially\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores\n    # IDF(t) = log(N / DF(t)) + 1 (smoothed)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n        num_workers=num_workers,\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_simple(\n    query: str, idf: dict, inverted_index: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, top_k: int, doc_titles: dict\n) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Simplified worker function to search a single query with minimal serialization overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n        idf: IDF scores dictionary\n        inverted_index: Inverted index\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        top_k: Number of top results\n        doc_titles: Document titles\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    top_results = scores[:top_k]\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # For small batches, parallelization overhead is too high\n    # Use sequential processing if we have fewer queries than workers\n    if len(queries) < num_workers * 2:\n        # Fall back to sequential processing\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n        results = [\n            _search_single_query_simple(\n                query, index.idf, index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, top_k, doc_titles\n            )\n            for query in queries\n        ]\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return (results, elapsed)\n\n    # Prepare document titles\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Create a partial function with fixed parameters to reduce serialization overhead\n    search_func = partial(\n        _search_single_query_simple,\n        idf=index.idf,\n        inverted_index=index.inverted_index,\n        doc_vectors=index.doc_vectors,\n        doc_norms=index.doc_norms,\n        top_k=top_k,\n        doc_titles=doc_titles,\n    )\n\n    # Process queries in parallel\n    # Use chunksize to reduce overhead for large batches\n    chunksize = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 4))\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(search_func, queries, chunksize=chunksize)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n    from sequential import (\n        batch_search_sequential,\n        build_tfidf_index_sequential,\n        search_sequential,\n    )\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--query\",\n        type=str,\n        default=\"machine learning algorithm\",\n        help=\"Search query\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--benchmark\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Run benchmark comparison\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    if args.benchmark:\n        # Benchmark mode: compare sequential vs parallel\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(\"BENCHMARK: Sequential vs Parallel\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Sequential indexing\n        print(\"\\n[Sequential] Building TF-IDF index...\")\n        seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n        print(f\"Index built in {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n\n        # Parallel indexing\n        print(f\"\\n[Parallel] Building TF-IDF index with {args.num_workers} workers...\")\n        par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n            documents, num_workers=args.num_workers\n        )\n        print(f\"Index built in {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n\n        speedup = seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time\n        print(f\"\\nIndexing speedup: {speedup:.2f}x\")\n\n        # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup over sequential index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup over sequential searching with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import partial\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Result Types\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n    num_workers: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(args: tuple) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, start_idx = args\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n\n\ndef _merge_vocabularies(vocab_list: list[set]) -> set:\n    \"\"\"Merge multiple vocabulary sets.\"\"\"\n    result = set()\n    for vocab in vocab_list:\n        result.update(vocab)\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_doc_data(data_list: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge multiple document data dictionaries.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for data in data_list:\n        result.update(data)\n    return result\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: Optional[int] = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Split documents into chunks for parallel processing\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel: tokenize and compute TF\n    3. Merge results and compute global document frequencies\n    4. Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    5. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (sequential - requires merged data)\n    6. Compute document vector norms (sequential - fast operation)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Adjust chunk size based on total documents to ensure good load balancing\n    # We want at least 2x num_workers chunks for good distribution\n    min_chunks = num_workers * 2\n    adjusted_chunk_size = max(1, len(documents) // min_chunks)\n    if chunk_size > adjusted_chunk_size and len(documents) > min_chunks:\n        chunk_size = adjusted_chunk_size\n\n    # Step 1: Split documents into chunks\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunk = documents[i : i + chunk_size]\n        chunks.append((chunk, i))\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel\n    # Use chunksize=1 to ensure all workers are utilized\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all workers\n    all_doc_term_freqs = []\n    all_doc_terms = []\n    all_vocabularies = []\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in results:\n        all_doc_term_freqs.append(doc_term_freqs)\n        all_doc_terms.append(doc_terms)\n        all_vocabularies.append(vocabulary)\n\n    # Merge all data\n    doc_term_freqs = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_term_freqs)\n    doc_terms = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_terms)\n    index.vocabulary = _merge_vocabularies(all_vocabularies)\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies\n    # This is fast enough to do sequentially\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores\n    # IDF(t) = log(N / DF(t)) + 1 (smoothed)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n        num_workers=num_workers,\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_simple(\n    query: str, idf: dict, inverted_index: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, top_k: int, doc_titles: dict\n) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Simplified worker function to search a single query with minimal serialization overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n        idf: IDF scores dictionary\n        inverted_index: Inverted index\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        top_k: Number of top results\n        doc_titles: Document titles\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    top_results = scores[:top_k]\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # For small batches, parallelization overhead is too high\n    # Use sequential processing if we have fewer queries than workers\n    if len(queries) < num_workers * 2:\n        # Fall back to sequential processing\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n        results = [\n            _search_single_query_simple(\n                query, index.idf, index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, top_k, doc_titles\n            )\n            for query in queries\n        ]\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return (results, elapsed)\n\n    # Prepare document titles\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Create a partial function with fixed parameters to reduce serialization overhead\n    search_func = partial(\n        _search_single_query_simple,\n        idf=index.idf,\n        inverted_index=index.inverted_index,\n        doc_vectors=index.doc_vectors,\n        doc_norms=index.doc_norms,\n        top_k=top_k,\n        doc_titles=doc_titles,\n    )\n\n    # Process queries in parallel\n    # Use chunksize to reduce overhead for large batches\n    chunksize = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 4))\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(search_func, queries, chunksize=chunksize)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n    from sequential import (\n        batch_search_sequential,\n        build_tfidf_index_sequential,\n        search_sequential,\n    )\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--query\",\n        type=str,\n        default=\"machine learning algorithm\",\n        help=\"Search query\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--benchmark\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Run benchmark comparison\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    if args.benchmark:\n        # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n\nPerformance targets:\n- 1.5x speedup over sequential index building with 4 workers\n- 2x speedup over sequential searching with 4 workers\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import partial\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom document_generator import Document\nfrom sequential import (\n    SearchResult,\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    tokenize,\n)\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Result Types\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n    num_workers: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_chunk(args: tuple) -> tuple:\n    \"\"\"\n    Worker function to process a chunk of documents.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, start_idx = args\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n\n\ndef _merge_vocabularies(vocab_list: list[set]) -> set:\n    \"\"\"Merge multiple vocabulary sets.\"\"\"\n    result = set()\n    for vocab in vocab_list:\n        result.update(vocab)\n    return result\n\n\ndef _merge_doc_data(data_list: list[dict]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Merge multiple document data dictionaries.\"\"\"\n    result = {}\n    for data in data_list:\n        result.update(data)\n    return result\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document], num_workers: Optional[int] = None, chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Split documents into chunks for parallel processing\n    2. Process each chunk in parallel: tokenize and compute TF\n    3. Merge results and compute global document frequencies\n    4. Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    5. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores (sequential - requires merged data)\n    6. Compute document vector norms (sequential - fast operation)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Adjust chunk size based on total documents to ensure good load balancing\n    # We want at least 2x num_workers chunks for good distribution\n    min_chunks = num_workers * 2\n    adjusted_chunk_size = max(1, len(documents) // min_chunks)\n    if chunk_size > adjusted_chunk_size and len(documents) > min_chunks:\n        chunk_size = adjusted_chunk_size\n\n    # Step 1: Split documents into chunks\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        chunk = documents[i : i + chunk_size]\n        chunks.append((chunk, i))\n\n    # Step 2: Process chunks in parallel\n    # Use chunksize=1 to ensure all workers are utilized\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_chunk, chunks, chunksize=1)\n\n    # Step 3: Merge results from all workers\n    all_doc_term_freqs = []\n    all_doc_terms = []\n    all_vocabularies = []\n\n    for doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary in results:\n        all_doc_term_freqs.append(doc_term_freqs)\n        all_doc_terms.append(doc_terms)\n        all_vocabularies.append(vocabulary)\n\n    # Merge all data\n    doc_term_freqs = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_term_freqs)\n    doc_terms = _merge_doc_data(all_doc_terms)\n    index.vocabulary = _merge_vocabularies(all_vocabularies)\n\n    # Step 4: Calculate document frequencies\n    # This is fast enough to do sequentially\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 5: Compute IDF scores\n    # IDF(t) = log(N / DF(t)) + 1 (smoothed)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 6: Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        posting_list = []\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    # Step 7: Build document vectors and compute norms\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        index.doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        index.doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary),\n        num_workers=num_workers,\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_single_query_simple(\n    query: str, idf: dict, inverted_index: dict, doc_vectors: dict, doc_norms: dict, top_k: int, doc_titles: dict\n) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Simplified worker function to search a single query with minimal serialization overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        query: Search query string\n        idf: IDF scores dictionary\n        inverted_index: Inverted index\n        doc_vectors: Document vectors\n        doc_norms: Document norms\n        top_k: Number of top results\n        doc_titles: Document titles\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in idf:\n            tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    top_results = scores[:top_k]\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: Optional[int] = None,\n    documents: Optional[list[Document]] = None,\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using multiprocessing.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # For small batches, parallelization overhead is too high\n    # Use sequential processing if we have fewer queries than workers\n    if len(queries) < num_workers * 2:\n        # Fall back to sequential processing\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n        results = [\n            _search_single_query_simple(\n                query, index.idf, index.inverted_index, index.doc_vectors, index.doc_norms, top_k, doc_titles\n            )\n            for query in queries\n        ]\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return (results, elapsed)\n\n    # Prepare document titles\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Create a partial function with fixed parameters to reduce serialization overhead\n    search_func = partial(\n        _search_single_query_simple,\n        idf=index.idf,\n        inverted_index=index.inverted_index,\n        doc_vectors=index.doc_vectors,\n        doc_norms=index.doc_norms,\n        top_k=top_k,\n        doc_titles=doc_titles,\n    )\n\n    # Process queries in parallel\n    # Use chunksize to reduce overhead for large batches\n    chunksize = max(1, len(queries) // (num_workers * 4))\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(search_func, queries, chunksize=chunksize)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point for Testing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n    from sequential import (\n        batch_search_sequential,\n        build_tfidf_index_sequential,\n        search_sequential,\n    )\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of worker processes\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--query\",\n        type=str,\n        default=\"machine learning algorithm\",\n        help=\"Search query\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--benchmark\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Run benchmark comparison\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    if args.benchmark:\n        # Benchmark mode: compare sequential vs parallel\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n        print(\"BENCHMARK: Sequential vs Parallel\")\n        print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n        # Sequential indexing\n        print(\"\\n[Sequential] Building TF-IDF index...\")\n        seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n        print(f\"Index built in {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n\n        #
